<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="2 Kings 18:1–21; 2 Chronicles 26:1–33; Isaiah 6:1"> The vision of Yesha’yahu &#91;Yehovah has saved&#93; the son of Amotz &#91;strong&#93;, which he saw concerning Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; in the days of ‘Uziyah &#91;my strength is Yehovah&#93;, Toram &#91;Yehovah is Perfect&#93;, Achaz &#91;he has grasped&#93;, &#91;and&#93; Hizkiyahu &#91;Yehovah is my strength&#93;, kings of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Deuteronomy 32:1; Deuteronomy 30:19; Micah 1:2"> Hear, O heavens, and give ear, O earth: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken, I have nourished and brought up children, and they have rebelled against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 8:7; Jeremiah 4:22; Isaiah 44:18"> The ox knows his owner, and the ass his master’s crib: &#91;but&#93; Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; does not know, my people does not consider.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Isaiah 5:24; Jeremiah 2:17; Jeremiah 2:13"> Ah sinful nation, a people laden with iniquity, a seed of evildoers, children that are corrupters: they have forsaken the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), they have provoked the Holy One of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; unto anger, they are gone away backward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Daniel 9:8–11; Ezekiel 24:13; Jeremiah 5:3"> Why should you2f be stricken anymore? you2f will revolt more and more: the whole head is sick, and the whole heart faint.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Jeremiah 6:14; Jeremiah 33:6; Gosel 9:12"> From the sole of the foot even unto the head &#91;there is&#93; no soundness in it; &#91;but&#93; wounds, and bruises, and putrefying sores: they have not been closed, neither bound up, neither mollified (be softened; appeased) with ointment.</span>
</div>

</div>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="1 Samuel 15:22; Amos 5:21; Jeremiah 6:20"> Behold, all his fellows shall be ashamed: and the workmen, they &#91;are&#93; of men: let them all be gathered together, let them stand up; &#91;yet&#93; they shall fear, &#91;and&#93; they shall be ashamed together.</span>
